> **CLOSED 2026-10-08 — arc archived.** Tasks without a completed entry were not run; superseded by the owner's stop rulings D-EU-148 / D-EU-149 (2026-10-08).

- **Slug:** `eu-floor-division-residual-2026-09-29` · **Date:** 2026-09-29 · **Author:** director (manager session) · **Executor:** fresh Sonnet sessions, one per task range.
- **Design pointers (read-only, never edited):** `docs/docs_ACTIVE/europeanLocations/rules/RULES_dwelling_layout_groups_nocore_2026-09-03.html` (the seven checks; step 5 "Absorb what is left over"; step 6 "Close"), `rules/RULES_dwelling_layout_scheme_nocore_2026-09-03.html`, `rules/EXAMPLE_dwelling_layout_validation_nocore_2026-09-03.md` §2, `STATE_european_locations_v5.md` §8 (`D-EU-92`, `D-EU-93`, `D-EU-107`, `D-EU-111`, `FINDING 258`, `FINDING 267`, `FINDING 269`), `debugs/DEBUG_floor-division-gap-and-clean-pipeline_2026-09-08.md`, `implementation/DONE/PLAN_eu-recut-95pct-2026-09-08.md` (§8 T03 `FINDING 267`, T08 final table).

- **Standing owner rulings this plan obeys, never reopens:** the declared dwelling count `k` is never changed (`D-EU-88` clause 1, `D-EU-92` clause 2); `MAX_FLAT_ASPECT = 2.5` and every check threshold stay frozen (`D-EU-89`, `D-EU-110`); plate-quality fixes are **per affected building only, never a full-batch re-cut or re-simulation** (`FINDING 258`, owner: *"maybe for some buildings we need to re-design and re-simulate but not all batch, for some"*).

Next free after this plan: `D-EU-121` / `FINDING 272`.

## 1. What this plan delivers (plain words)

Of the 4,171 simulated residential buildings in the four neighbourhoods, **3,111 already follow every rule** (3,095 divided and clean, plus 16 whose data say one flat per floor, so one zone per floor is correct). The rest fall in three groups:

1. **Not divided at all — 310 buildings.** 209 had a good cut that was thrown away at the last IDF step (an extra vertex created while pairing surfaces, `FINDING 267`); 51 declare more than 12 flats per floor and the IDF engine refuses them *before trying* (the rule bench tries them, `D-EU-92`; the engine never got that change); 49 were cut but the cut breaks a hard rule (a flat in two pieces `C4`, or a flat with a hole / over 40 points `C5`); 1 other.
2. **Divided but breaking a rule — 310 buildings.** 204 are "best effort" (a flat too thin, too pinched or with too little façade, `D-EU-111`); 106 have one flat less than half the size of the largest on the same floor (balance rule, `D-EU-107`, the `FINDING 258` picture — screenshot 3).
3. **Divided with a wrapped flat — 415 buildings, to be checked.** A party wall bends inside the plate, so one flat wraps round its neighbour (the D8 / D9 picture). The rules say the wall between two flats is one straight line, except where it genuinely bends round a wing; T01 separates the two.

After this plan: the 209 lost cuts are recovered by an IDF repair, the 51 dense buildings are cut and judged like every other plate, and every badly divided building that **has a strictly better cut** gets it — re-emitted and re-simulated alone. Nothing that is clean today is touched. Expected end state is **measured at CP-2, never assumed**.

Director pre-measurement (2026-09-29, from `EU-11/<D>_merged_2026-09-08/<d>_manifest.csv`, `<D>_recut_2026-09-08/prepared_buildings.csv` and the installed side-cars `outputs_3D/eu_<D>_data/layouts/`), one class per building, first match wins:

The six screenshots, today: `BATIMENT…880367_part0` (Lyon, screenshot 3) → V1, balance 0.27, still the one-huge-flat picture; `BATIMENT…879459_part0` (Lyon, 4) → U2, 14 flats/floor; `29417`, `30090` (Bologna, 5–6) → U3, `C5`, 2 flats/floor on 2,444 / 5,393 m² plates; `way/388485201` (Madrid, not London, 7) → U2, 18 flats/floor; `way/333138130` (Madrid, 8) → already re-cut balanced by the 2026-09-08 recut (spread 0.67, the D8/D9 sliver gone) but still one wrapped corner → V3.

Screenshot 8's `C10 25.29 m²` FAIL is that stale page; the simulated IDF has a different, balanced cut. The `outputs_3D/` viewers are current; the `plans3D/` pages are not. T07 regenerates them.

## 2. Hard rules for the executor

1. **Model:** every executor session is Sonnet. Never plan, never propose alternatives; on ambiguity STOP and quote the conflicting lines.
2. **No cluster access.** Never run `ssh`, `scp`, `sbatch`, `srun`, `squeue`, `sacct`. Speed work (T06) is director-only.
3. **No local EnergyPlus, ever (owner, 2026-09-29).** Every EnergyPlus run goes to Speed as one `sbatch --array ...%32 --time=7-00:00:00`; the manager submits it, the executor only prepares the IDF list and stops.
4. **Four districts always run as four concurrent processes**, never one after another.
5. **Python:** `C:/Users/o_iseri/Desktop/OpenUBEM/.venv/Scripts/python.exe`. Tests: `.venv/Scripts/python.exe -m pytest tests/<file> -q`.
6. **Never edit** root `main.py`, any OVERVIEW/DESIGN doc, `docs/docs_ACTIVE/europeanLocations/rules/*`, `openubem/idf/surfaces.py` (`D-EU-41`), any check threshold, `MAX_FLAT_ASPECT`, `MIN_SPREAD`, `RULED_GRID_MAX_DWELLINGS_PER_FLOOR` (the constant stays; T03 only stops the nocore path from consulting it), `NEAR_DUPLICATE_VERTEX_TOLERANCE_M`, `COLLINEAR_VERTEX_ANGLE_TOLERANCE_DEG`, the `RING_VERTEX_REMOVAL_*` budgets.
   *Amended by director 2026-09-30 (`D-EU-125`):* `surfaces.py` was edited once by T04g (the OPEN-66 guard narrowed to zones flagged before the first extrusion). No further edit is allowed.
7. **Never change a dwelling count**, never redistribute dwellings across storeys, never name a building id in engine code (`D-EU-86`).
8. **Never delete or regenerate a delivered tree.** New output goes to new dated folders `*_fix_2026-09-29`, `*_merged_2026-09-29`, `*_layouts_2026-09-29`. Every `_recut_2026-09-08` / `_merged_2026-09-08` tree is a read-only input.
9. **A building that is OK today is never re-emitted.** Only the ids in `fix_candidates.csv` (T05) enter the new tree.
10. **Read guard:** `grep -n` first, then a ranged read; never read a whole file over 300 lines.
11. **Before debugging any error**, search `docs/docs_EXPLANATION/OpenUBEM_debug_References.md`; after solving one, register it there in house format before closing the task.
12. **Every count is "N of M"**, never a bare percentage.
13. **Progress log** (§8): one entry per task, `#### TXX — <title> — completed YYYY-MM-DD`, Artifacts / Deviations / Test status / Notes. Append only.
14. Figures → `openubem/outputs/` (flat). No `.py` under `docs/`. No code comments unless unreadable without one. No new scripts beyond the ones named here.

## 4. Dependency decisions (pinned)

1. **`D-EU-118` — mirror repair of paired surfaces (U1).** After `intersect_match` and the existing `_repair_*` / `_pair_interfloor_surfaces` steps, before the at-risk gate: for each `BUILDINGSURFACE:DETAILED` pair with `Outside_Boundary_Condition == Surface` (each pair once, lower name first), clean the first ring (drop a vertex closer than `NEAR_DUPLICATE_VERTEX_TOLERANCE_M` to its predecessor, drop a vertex whose turn is within `COLLINEAR_VERTEX_ANGLE_TOLERANCE_DEG` of straight), check the partner covers the same polygon (in the surface's own plane, symmetric-difference area ≤ 1 × 10⁻⁴ m² and every partner vertex within 0.005 m of the cleaned ring's boundary), then write the partner's vertices as the cleaned ring **reversed**. A pair that fails the coverage check is left untouched, and the existing gate still fires on it. Rationale: `FINDING 267` shows every U1 defect is a vertex `intersect_match` creates on one side of a pair and EnergyPlus removes asymmetrically; two rings that are exact reverses of each other cannot collapse differently.
2. **`D-EU-119` — `D-EU-92` in the engine (U2).** Under `EUROPEAN_LAYOUT_REGIME == "nocore"` only, the building-level density refusal (`:2682-2688`) is skipped; every storey goes to `generate_european_nocore_storey_layout` at its declared `k`. PASS → emitted; soft-only fail → best effort (`D-EU-111`); hard fail → box with `NOCORE_CHECK_FAILED_*`. The token `DWELLING_DENSITY_EXCEEDS_RULED_GRID_GT_12` no longer appears on the nocore path; every other regime is unchanged.
3. **`D-EU-120` — strictly-better per-building re-cut (V1, V2, V3-wrap, U3).** Two post-passes run on the chosen cut, each accepted only if the result is strictly better:
   - **Straighten:** a party wall that bends at a cut-made corner is replaced by the straight segment between its two ends on the plate boundary; leftover slivers go through the existing `donate_leftovers` rule. A bend whose wall starts at a footprint reflex vertex (≤ 0.10 m) is a wing bend and is never straightened (rules, groups sheet step 5: *"A wall that genuinely bends round a wing is kept as it is"*).
   - **Balance:** each straight party wall between two flats is slid along its own normal (1-D search, 0.05 m steps, within the two flats) to bring their areas closer; the equal-area intent of `equal_area_x` restored after donation.
   - **Strictly better** = passes every one of `C1 C3 C4 C5 C6 C10 C11` that the current cut passes, **and** improves at least one of {number of failed checks, `C12` spread, `C13` bent-wall count} without worsening another. Otherwise the current cut stays, byte-identical.
4. **`C13` — straight party walls, measured and reported, never a veto.** `C13` = number of cut-made inside corners that are not wing bends (definition of item 3). It is shown on the card beside `C12`, never folds into `rec["verdict"]`, same status as `C12` (`european_nocore.py:1734-1744`). It measures the existing rule of the groups sheet step 5; it adds no new threshold.
5. **Not in scope:** changing `k` (`FINDING 271` is recorded only); the 16 `k = 1` buildings (correct by the data, `D-EU-93` logic); the 25 without a side-car (`FINDING 269`, closed by `D-EU-117`).
6. **Regression gate:** every building not in `fix_candidates.csv` keeps its `_merged_2026-09-08` IDF hash; every candidate either improves or keeps its `_recut_2026-09-08` IDF hash. One regression fails T05.
   *Amended by director 2026-09-30:* since T04d–T04h clean every surface ring, IDF bytes differ on buildings whose geometry did not change, so the hash test is replaced for non-candidates by: same `geometry_outcome`, same class, same zone count, every zone floor area within 0.01 m² of the `_merged_2026-09-08` IDF, `at_risk` False, and 0 surfaces flagged by T04h's EnergyPlus-rule scan. A non-candidate that fails any of these is a regression. Candidates must improve their class, or keep it with a different `idf_sha256`.
7. **Speed:** four fleets (fix ids only), four arrays in the same minute, each `--array=1-N%32 --time=7-00:00:00 -p ps`; ETA from `sacct`, never guessed.
8. **Published numbers:** the 2026-09-08 EUIs stay the quotable figures until CP-3. A restatement is the owner's call, and GSSCanada/4J is told by the director before and after (4J froze against the 2026-09-08 populations).

## 5. Facts with line citations (verified 2026-09-29)

1. The IDF engine refuses `k > 12` before any cut: `european_residential.py:2682-2688` returns `dwelling_layout_emitted=False` with `DWELLING_DENSITY_REFUSAL_TOKEN` (`:34`). The bench tries them (`scripts/eu21/08_district_viewer.py:165-174`, `"cut attempted at declared k and failed the seven checks (D-EU-92)"`) — the two disagree, which is why screenshots 4 and 7 say "attempted" while the IDF never tried.
2. The nocore storey path does not consult the 12 cap: `generate_european_nocore_storey_layout` (`:1338-1412`) calls `cut_storey_nocore` (`european_nocore.py:1888`) at any `k`; the `ValueError` guards at `:310`, `:916`, `:1016`, `:1563`, `:1640`, `:1694`, `:1822` belong to the ruled/group routes only.
3. Best effort is emitted when the failed set ⊆ {`C6`,`C10`,`C11`} and the flat count matches (`:1386-1392`); `C1`/`C3`/`C4`/`C5` refuse (`:1393-1396`).
4. `_plate_score` ranks `spread` (C12) after cov/C5/C6/C4/C11/C10 (`european_nocore.py:1187`), so an unbalanced cut wins whenever a balanced one fails any earlier gate — the mechanism behind V2 and screenshot 3.
5. The at-risk gate and reroute: `run_eu_s2_campaign.py:680-718` — `_has_near_duplicate_vertex_surfaces(idf)` → `_force_reroute_room_layout_to_one_zone_per_floor` deletes the cut. `FINDING 267` (`PLAN_eu-recut-95pct-2026-09-08.md:262`): for all 184 measured then, `refused_by == not_on_ring` — the vertex is created by `intersect_match`, after every ring cleanup. CP-1 of that plan named "a post-`intersect_match` vertex merge or a per-pair surface repair" as the only class of remedy that could act on it (`:273`).
6. Tolerances: `NEAR_DUPLICATE_VERTEX_TOLERANCE_M = 0.005` (`run_eu_s2_campaign.py:77`), `COLLINEAR_VERTEX_ANGLE_TOLERANCE_DEG = 0.1` (`:91`).
7. Wrapped flats come from leftover donation: `donate_leftovers` (`european_nocore.py:494`) and `_delobe_and_donate` (`:557`) hand pieces to the neighbour with the most shared wall, which can turn a rectangle into an L (screenshot 8, D8 around D9/D10).
8. Harvest tags: `harvest_eu11_merged.py:21-24`; viewer tag chains `generate_eu_3d_viewers.py:860`, `:1087`.
9. Wall-B measurement tree (reuse for T02): `EU-11/wallB_second_pass_2026-09-08/wallB_defects.csv` (184 rows).

## 6. Tasks

### T01 — One census of every simulated building: status, cause, and the V3 split

**What.** Write `scripts/analysis/eu_division_residual_census_2026-09-29.py`. For each of the 4,171 rows of the four `_merged_2026-09-08` manifests, emit one row of `division_status.csv`: `district, building_id, stem, geometry_outcome, fallback_reason, k_max, storeys, dwellings, floor_area_m2, m2_per_dwelling, min_spread, c13_bent_walls, wing_bends, max_zone_vertices, class`. Classes exactly as §1's table (first match wins). Then: (a) `u3_causes.csv` — for the 49 U3, which part of `C4`/`C5` fails (`hole`, `contains`, `points_gt_40`, `lobed`, `overlap`); (b) `v3_split.csv` — the 415 V3 split into `wrap` (≥ 1 corner that is not a wing bend) and `wing_bend_only`; (c) `histogram.json` of class × district.
**Why.** §1's numbers are a director pre-measurement; the plan needs one reproducible file, and V3 mixes allowed wing bends with real wraps.
**How.** Read-only. Side-cars from `outputs_3D/eu_<D>_data/layouts/<building_id>.json`; `fallback_reason` from `_recut_2026-09-08/prepared_buildings.csv`. Wing bend = the bent wall's run starts at a footprint reflex vertex within 0.10 m (reflex = interior angle > 180° after `REFLEX_DENOISE_TOLERANCE_M` denoise, `european_nocore.py:1294`). Four processes.
**How to test.** Row count 4,171; class counts equal §1 to ±0 (any difference is reported with the ids, not smoothed); `wrap + wing_bend_only == 415`; the six screenshot ids land in the classes §1 names. Register `FINDING 270` and `FINDING 271` in §8 with the measured numbers. **STOP → CP-1.**

### T02 — Mirror repair of paired surfaces (`D-EU-118`)

**What.** Add `_mirror_interzone_pairs(idf) -> int` (returns pairs rewritten) to `scripts/run_eu_s2_campaign.py`, called once immediately before `mismatched = find_mismatched_interzone_pairs(idf)` (`:674`). Spec: §4 item 1.
**Why.** Recovers U1 (209) without touching the cut, the footprint or any rule.
**How.** Pure IDF-object edit (eppy vertex fields), no geometry library call inside the loop except the coverage check (shapely in the surface's own 2-D frame). Log per building: `pairs_seen, pairs_rewritten, pairs_skipped_coverage`.
**How to test.** (1) Unit tests in the new test file: a synthetic pair with an injected near-duplicate vertex on one side comes out as exact reverses; a pair that does not cover the same polygon is left untouched. (2) Rebuild the 209 U1 IDFs locally **without simulating** (same `build_idf_for_building` call, scratch `run_dir` under `division_residual_2026-09-29/scratch/`): report `at_risk` before/after as N of 209. (3) Rebuild 100 random OK buildings (seed 20260929): `at_risk` stays False on all, zone count unchanged. (4) EnergyPlus proof, local pool ≤ 20: 20 of the recovered IDFs with `SimulationControl` sizing-only; report fatal count (target 0) and quote any `** Severe **` verbatim. `tests/test_eu_collinear_ring_cleanup.py` and `tests/test_eu_recut_95pct_2026_09_08.py` stay green.

### T03 — `D-EU-92` in the engine (`D-EU-119`)

**What.** In `generate_european_building_dwelling_layout` (`european_residential.py:2682`), apply the density refusal only when `EUROPEAN_LAYOUT_REGIME != "nocore"`.
**Why.** U2 (51) are refused before any cut; the owner ruled they must be cut and judged (`D-EU-92`).
**How.** One condition; nothing else in the function changes.
**How to test.** Dry run of the 51 U2 ids through the layout call only (no IDF): report PASS / best effort (with failed ids) / hard fail (with failed ids) as N of 51. A unit test: `k = 14` on a 20 × 20 m square returns a layout that is either emitted or refused with `NOCORE_CHECK_FAILED_*`, never the density token. Non-nocore regime: density refusal still returned.

### T02b — Drop sliver surfaces after the mirror (`D-EU-121`, added by director after T02)

**What.** Add `_drop_sliver_surfaces(idf, max_area_m2=0.01, max_total_m2=0.1) -> tuple[int, float]` to `scripts/run_eu_s2_campaign.py`, called once immediately after `_mirror_interzone_pairs(idf)` (`:840`). It removes every `BUILDINGSURFACE:DETAILED` whose Newell area is `< max_area_m2` or whose cleaned ring (`_clean_ring_3d`) has fewer than 3 distinct vertices, together with every member of its linked interzone group (`_interzone_groups`) and every `FENESTRATIONSURFACE:DETAILED` hosted on a removed surface. If the building's total removed area would exceed `max_total_m2`, it removes nothing and returns `(0, 0.0)`. Returns `(surfaces_removed, area_removed_m2)`.
**Why.** T02 §8 and the two debug entries it registered: 195 of 209 U1 stay at risk only because `intersect_match` leaves sliver triangles of 1e-4 to 1e-3 m² that no vertex cleaning can remove, and one recovered IDF (`eb12e1ba00541e12`) is fatal on a 2.5e-10 m² floor. A surface under 10 × 10 cm carries no measurable heat flow.
**How.** IDF object removal only (`idf.removeidfobject`); no geometry library call. Log per building: `slivers_removed, sliver_area_m2`, appended to the T02 scratch CSVs as two new columns.
**How to test.** (1) Unit tests in the T02 test file: a sliver interzone pair is removed on both sides; a 0.02 m² surface is kept; a building whose slivers total over 0.1 m² keeps everything. (2) Rebuild the 209 U1 again (same scratch layout, new `u1c/` and `u1c_result.csv`): `at_risk` after, as N of 209 per district. (3) The same 100 OK buildings: `at_risk` False on 100 of 100, zone count unchanged, plus how many had slivers removed and the largest removed area. (4) EnergyPlus proof with T02's exact settings on **every** recovered U1 IDF, run on Speed by the manager (executor writes the IDF list to `scratch/u1c_ep_list.txt` and stops; no local EnergyPlus): fatal count, each fatal id listed with its `** Severe **` line verbatim; fatal ids are excluded from T05. (5) The T02 test list stays green. Register every error met in the debug references; drop `[OPEN]` from the `eb12e1ba00541e12` entry if it now runs.

### T04 — Straighten and balance, strictly better only (`D-EU-120`, `C13`)

**What.** In `european_nocore.py`: add `c13_bent_walls(poly, flats) -> int` and `_straighten_walls(poly, flats)`, `_balance_walls(poly, flats)`; add `C13` to `run_checks` (reported, not in `verdict`, like `C12`). In `generate_european_nocore_storey_layout` (`european_residential.py:1338`), after `cut_storey_nocore`: try straighten, then balance, each kept only if strictly better (§4 item 3); also re-evaluate the best-effort / refusal branch on the kept result.
**Why.** V2 (106), V3-wrap (T01), and part of V1 (204) and U3 (49) are cuts the rules describe differently from what was drawn (groups sheet step 5: one straight wall, equal-area intent).
**How.** Straighten works in the plate frame from `frame()` (`:137`); a wall's two ends are the points where it meets the plate exterior; if either end is interior (a three-flat junction), skip that wall. Balance moves one wall at a time, re-running `run_checks` on the two flats it touches, max 40 steps per wall.
**How to test.** Unit tests: (a) an L-shaped flat wrapped round a neighbour on a rectangle plate comes out as two rectangles with equal-or-better checks; (b) a wing-bend wall on an L plate is left untouched; (c) a cut that is already clean comes back byte-identical. Dry run over every class U3/V1/V2/V3 id (layout call only, four processes): report per class and district `improved / unchanged / would_regress (must be 0)`, and the new check tally. **STOP → CP-2 is after T05.**

### T04c — Why each remaining home still fails (owner, 2026-09-29: "we need to solve these" before any simulation)

**What.** Measure, for every home still failing after T02b/T03/T04, whether a rule-passing division exists at all or which frozen rule makes it impossible. Populations from the director's projection `division_after_2026-09-29.csv` (copy it to `division_residual_2026-09-29/`): 88 lost-cut homes still sent to one zone per floor (`u1c_result.csv` `rerouted == True`), 49 U3, 1 U4, 242 V1 (204 V1 + 38 former U2), 98 V2. Two outputs in `division_residual_2026-09-29/`:
1. `residual_why_geometry.csv`, one row per (building, storey) for U3/U4/V1/V2: `district, building_id, after_class, storey, k, plate_m2, exterior_m, m2_per_flat, failed_chosen, n_candidates, n_pass_all_gates, n_pass_all_gates_and_spread, best_c4_ok, best_c5_ok, best_c6_m, best_c10_m2, best_c11_aspect, best_spread, c6_impossible, verdict`. `best_*` = best value reached over **every** candidate `build_flats` scores (not only the chosen one). `c6_impossible` = `exterior_m / k < 2.50`. `verdict` (first match): `A_pass_exists_not_chosen` (n_pass_all_gates > 0), `B_rule_impossible` (c6_impossible), `C_near_miss` (every failing check within 10 % of its threshold at its best candidate: C6 ≥ 2.25 m, C11 ≤ 2.75, C10 ≤ 0.11 m², spread ≥ 0.45), `D_cutter_lacks_move` (C4 or C5 fail on every candidate), `E_far`.
2. `residual_why_pairs.csv`, one row per skipped paired-surface group of the 88: `district, building_id, surface_a, surface_b, area_a, area_b, area_symdiff, a_contains_b, b_contains_a, n_vert_a, n_vert_b, verdict` with `verdict` (first match) `same_polygon_extra_vertex` (symdiff ≤ 0.01 m²), `one_side_larger` (one contains the other), `partial_overlap`.
Plus `residual_why_summary.md`: verdict × after_class × district counts, N of M.

**Why.** Before any new cutter move or any simulation, the owner needs to know which failures a better cutter can fix and which are blocked by a frozen rule (flat count, `MAX_FLAT_ASPECT`, `C6`), so fixes target only what is fixable.

**How.** Read-only; engine code untouched. One scratch script `scratch/t04c_why.py`, built from `scratch/t04_dry_run.py` (same footprint, side-car and allocation loading). Record candidates by wrapping `nc._plate_score` at run time (monkeypatch in the script, as `t04_dry_run.py` wraps `cut_storey_nocore`), calling `run_checks` on each scored `(poly, flats)`. Pairs: rebuild the 88 IDFs exactly as T02b did (`u1c` path) into `scratch/u1d/` and, at the point `_mirror_interzone_pairs_stats` counts a coverage skip, measure the two rings in the surface's own 2-D frame with shapely. Four district processes at once, at most 10 local processes in total. No EnergyPlus.

**How to test.** Row totals: geometry rows cover 390 of 390 buildings (49 + 1 + 242 + 98), pairs cover 88 of 88 buildings and the skipped-group count equals `u1c_result.csv` `pairs_skipped_coverage` summed over those 88; `n_pass_all_gates > 0` rows listed by id (expected 0 — any hit is an engine selection defect, reported, not fixed). **STOP → CP-1b.**

### T04d — Repair the paired surfaces that still send 92 homes to one zone per floor (`D-EU-122`, director, CP-1b)

**What.** In `scripts/run_eu_s2_campaign.py` only, extend the repair that runs before `find_mismatched_interzone_pairs(idf)` (`:883-885`) so the 92 lost-cut homes (88 rerouted + 4 fatal in the T02b proof) keep their flats:
1. `same_polygon_extra_vertex` (215 groups, T04c): when `_pair_covers_same_polygon` (`:204`) fails but the symmetric difference is ≤ `MIRROR_NEAR_SAME_AREA_M2 = 0.01` m², mirror the group to the cleaned ring of its larger-area member instead of skipping it.
2. `one_side_larger` (160 groups): split the larger surface L into `L ∩ S` (mirrored exactly with the smaller S) and the remainder pieces `L − S`. Each remainder piece: area < 0.01 m² → dropped (same size as `D-EU-121`); covered to within 0.01 m² by one coplanar, opposite-facing, not-yet-paired surface of another zone → paired with it by exact mirror; otherwise `Outside_Boundary_Condition = Adiabatic`. Skip the group unchanged (count it) when L hosts a fenestration surface, when a piece has a hole, or when the building's total adiabatic area would exceed `MAX_ADIABATIC_REMAINDER_M2 = 1.0`.
3. `degenerate_ring` (4 groups): remove through `_drop_sliver_surfaces` (`:321`) when the group is under its limits; otherwise leave and count.
4. Every `BUILDINGSURFACE:DETAILED` ring, paired or not, goes through `_clean_ring_3d` (`:148`) once, so no surface keeps two consecutive equal vertices.
**Why.** T04c §8: 84 of 88 rerouted homes carry 379 skipped pair groups, 0 of them `partial_overlap`, so every one is a pair whose two sides are the same wall or one wall inside the other. The 4 fatal proof homes (T02b proof on Speed, `eplusout.err` verbatim):
- Madrid `2542f2907e276cf1`: `** Severe  ** CalcCoordinateTransformation: Invalid dot product, surface="BLOCK 2542F2907E276CF1_2 STOREY 0 FLOOR 0001_5":` with vertices `(440155.221,4479250.206,   0.000)` listed twice in a row.
- Bologna `d1a9087437c27794`: same message on `"BLOCK D1A9087437C27794_1 STOREY 0 CEILING 0001_3"`, vertex `(687142.134,4928764.013,   3.000)` twice in a row.
- Madrid `803d0afdbbbb83e9`: `** Severe  ** RoofCeiling:Detailed="BLOCK 803D0AFDBBBB83E9_1 STOREY 0 CEILING 0001_5", Vertex size mismatch between base surface :BLOCK 803D0AFDBBBB83E9_1 STOREY 0 CEILING 0001_5 and outside boundary surface: BLOCK 803D0AFDBBBB83E9_1 STOREY 1 FLOOR 0001_1` / `The vertex sizes are 7 for base surface and 6 for outside boundary surface.`
- Madrid `c5e730d9efda6799`: the same mismatch on six ceiling/floor pairs, 14 vs 15 vertices.
A mismatch on a single wall between two conditioned flats carries no measurable heat flow; losing the whole floor's flats for it does.
**How.** IDF object edits (eppy) plus shapely in the surface's own 2-D frame (`_plane_frame`), exactly as `_pair_covers_same_polygon` already does. New counts per building in the result CSV: `pairs_near_same_mirrored, pairs_split, remainder_paired, remainder_dropped, remainder_adiabatic, adiabatic_area_m2, pairs_still_skipped`. For each of the 4 fatal ids, find which step leaves the named surface as it is, and fix that step; quote the step in §8. For the 4 rerouted homes with no skipped group, report the reroute reason (`interzone_vertex_mismatch` or `near_duplicate_vertex`) and the surface that trips it.
**How to test.** (1) Unit tests appended to `tests/test_eu_floor_division_residual_2026_09_29.py`: extra-vertex pair mirrored; one-side-larger pair split with the remainder paired, dropped and adiabatic in three cases; a pair over the adiabatic cap left unchanged; a floor ring with a doubled vertex comes out with it removed. (2) Rebuild the 92 without simulating into `scratch/u1e/`, result `scratch/u1e_result.csv`: `rerouted` and `at_risk` after, N of 92 per district; ids still rerouted listed with reason. (3) The same 100 OK buildings as T02 (seed 20260929): `at_risk` False on 100 of 100, zone count unchanged, and how many had any surface changed. (4) Write every recovered IDF path to `scratch/u1e_ep_list.txt` and stop; the director runs the EnergyPlus proof on Speed. At most **5** local processes (T04e runs at the same time). No EnergyPlus. The T02 test list stays green. Register the two fatal signatures above in `docs/docs_EXPLANATION/OpenUBEM_debug_References.md` (house format, cause and fix line).

### T04e — Cutter moves for near misses and over-long outlines (`D-EU-123`, director, CP-1b)

**What.** In `openubem/geometry/european_nocore.py` only, inside `improve_cut_nocore` (`:2232`), after `_balance_pass`, add three steps, each kept only if `_strictly_better` (`:2031`) and each returning the very same objects when nothing is better:
1. `_shape_pass(poly, flats, k, base)`: when any of C6, C10, C11 or C12 fails, for up to `SHAPE_MAX_ROUNDS = 12` rounds, for each party wall (`_party_walls`, `:2059`) touching a flat that fails C6/C10/C11 (or the smallest/largest flat for C12), try the first `SHAPE_TOP_CANDIDATES = 8` of `_slid_variants` (`:2152`) and every `_straighten_candidates` (`:2097`) variant; keep the best strictly better one; stop when a round accepts nothing.
2. `_simplify_pass(poly, flats, k, base)`: when C5 fails only on point count (> 40, no hole, no containment), re-run `build_flats` (`:1489`) on the plate simplified with `simplify(tol, preserve_topology=True)` for `tol` in `0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.30` m; accept the first cut whose C1 passes against the **original** plate and that is strictly better.
3. `_wide_search_pass(poly, flats, k, base)`: when the cut still fails any gated check after steps 1–2, re-run `build_flats` with `MAX_THETA_OFFSETS` (`:1292`) raised to `WIDE_THETA_OFFSETS = 24` for this call only, then steps 1–2 on its result; keep only if strictly better.
**Why.** T04c §8: no cut the cutter already scores passes and goes unchosen (`A` = 0), so only new moves can help. 573 failing storeys are near misses (mostly C10 pinch 198, C11 aspect 199, C12 balance 81), and the median `E_far` storey's best aspect is 2.76 against a limit of 2.5, so the same moves reach part of `E` too. The 226 `D` storeys are one or two huge flats per floor (median plate 1,737 m², 117 of them `k = 1`): C5 fails on 182 (hole, containment or > 40 points), C4 on 44 (the single flat is itself lobed).
**How.** No change to any frozen rule: dwelling count, `k`, `MAX_FLAT_ASPECT`, `MIN_SPREAD`, every check threshold stay as they are; `run_checks` is not edited. Where a storey cannot pass with `k` as it is (a `k = 1` flat with a courtyard hole, a `k = 1` flat that is lobed, `B_rule_impossible`), do nothing and list it.
**How to test.** (1) Unit tests in the new file `tests/test_eu_cutter_moves_2026_09_29.py` (T04d appends to the residual test file at the same time): a thin-flat cut on a rectangle that slides to C11 ≤ 2.5; a 60-point `k = 1` plate that simplifies to ≤ 40 points with C1 still passing; a clean cut comes back as the same objects. (2) Dry run, layout call only, with `scratch/t04c_why.py`'s loading, over U3/U4/V1/V2 (390) **and** the V3 ids (414), at most **5** local processes: per class and district `improved / unchanged / regressed (must be 0)`; new after-class counts; residual storeys listed in `scratch/t04e_residual.csv` with one reason each: `k1_hole`, `k1_lobed`, `rule_impossible_c6`, `still_near_miss`, `still_far`. (3) The 100 OK buildings (seed 20260929): every cut unchanged, compared object by object. The T04 test list stays green. **Then T04d + T04e → CP-1c.**

**What.** Three items, in order. Code changes in `scripts/run_eu_s2_campaign.py` only; `european_nocore.py` is read, not edited, unless item 1 names a T04e step (then see item 1).

2. **Remainder with nothing across it.** In `_split_larger_group`, before a remainder piece becomes Adiabatic, test whether any surface of any other zone of the building lies within 0.01 m of the piece's plane, faces the other way and overlaps the piece by more than 0.01 m². If none does, the piece is a real outside surface (example in T04d §8: Madrid `way/942352870`, a 54.72 m² ceiling under a 0.0 m² floor): a `Ceiling` piece becomes `Roof`, `Outdoors`, `SunExposed`, `WindExposed`, with the construction of an existing `Roof` surface of the same IDF; a `Floor` piece becomes `Outdoors`, `NoSun`, `WindExposed`, with the construction of an existing outdoor or ground `Floor` of the same IDF. If the IDF has no such surface to copy the construction from, keep the current Adiabatic path. Outside pieces do not count toward `MAX_ADIABATIC_REMAINDER_M2 = 1.0`, which stays. New CSV column `remainder_outdoors`, `outdoors_area_m2`.

The OK-100 change must be explained before any building is re-emitted in T05.
**How.** Same eppy + shapely frame as T04d. No frozen rule changes (dwelling count, `k`, `MAX_FLAT_ASPECT`, `MIN_SPREAD`, C6, every check threshold). No EnergyPlus, no cluster, at most **5** local processes.
**How to test.** (1) Unit tests appended to `tests/test_eu_floor_division_residual_2026_09_29.py`: a ceiling remainder with nothing above becomes a `Roof` with the copied construction; a remainder with a surface above stays Adiabatic; a building with 60 slivers totalling 0.2 m² has them dropped; a pair with symmetric difference exactly 0.01 m² is mirrored. (2) Rebuild the 92 into `scratch/u1f/`, result `scratch/u1f_result.csv`: recovered N of 92 per district; every id still rerouted with reason. (3) OK-100 from item 1: `at_risk` False and zone count equal to `ok100c` on 100 of 100, or each exception named with its cause. (4) `scratch/u1f_ep_list.txt` = every recovered IDF whose `idf_sha256` differs from its `u1e` row or that was not recovered in `u1e`; stop, the director runs the proof on Speed. ### T04g — Narrow the re-extrusion guard, remove ring spikes (`D-EU-125`, director, T04f audit)

**What.** Two code changes, then one rebuild.
1. **Guard.** `openubem/idf/surfaces.py:940-941` (added 2026-09-29 by `openings/implemenation/previous/PLAN_ten-items-close-2026-09-29.md` T04, OPEN-66) raises whenever any zone carries `extruded`. But `_extrude_zone_groups` itself sets `z["extruded"] = True` on every zone it extrudes (`surfaces.py:867`), so by the time of the `match()`-alone retry every zone is flagged and the retry never runs for any building, not only for core/perim ones. Compute, before the first `_extrude_zone_groups(idf, valid_zones, _exc)` call in `extrude_geometry`, whether any zone was flagged `extruded` at that moment (the core/perim expanded zones, which `valid_zones` skips); the guard raises only on that value. Nothing else in `surfaces.py` changes. This is the only edit to `surfaces.py` (director ruling `D-EU-125`: it restores the pre-guard behaviour for every building whose zones are all in `valid_zones`, and keeps OPEN-66's intent for core/perim ones).
2. **Spikes.** In `scripts/run_eu_s2_campaign.py`, `_clean_ring_3d` (`:148`) also removes out-and-back spikes: while some vertex `P[i]` has `P[i-1]` and `P[i+1]` within `RING_CLEAN_MIN_EDGE_M` of each other, drop `P[i]` and `P[i+1]`; repeat until none is left or fewer than 3 vertices remain (then keep the input ring). Evidence (T04d Speed proof, Madrid `2542f2907e276cf1`, `u1e` IDF, `Block 2542f2907e276cf1_2 Storey 0 Floor 0001_5`): the ring runs `(440155.885,4479250.600) → (440155.221,4479250.206) → (440155.991,4479248.945) → (440155.221,4479250.206) → (440155.885,4479250.600)`; EnergyPlus deletes the collinear points and stops with `** Severe  ** CalcCoordinateTransformation: Invalid dot product, surface="BLOCK 2542F2907E276CF1_2 STOREY 0 FLOOR 0001_5":` with `(440155.221,4479250.206,   0.000)` twice in a row. T04d's gate counted 0 doubled vertices on it, because the doubling only appears after EnergyPlus's own collinear deletion.
3. **Rebuild.** The 92 into `scratch/u1g/` (result `scratch/u1g_result.csv`) and the OK-100 into `scratch/ok100g/` (result `scratch/ok100g_result.csv`).
**Why.** T04f §8: the guard alone sends 4 of the 100 OK buildings to one zone per floor or to a failed build; the same guard can hold back part of the 48 still rerouted. The spike ring is the one fatal left in the T04d proof.
**How.** No frozen EU rule changes. No EnergyPlus, no cluster, at most **5** local processes. Reuse `scratch/t04d_rebuild.py`.
**How to test.** (1) `tests/test_builder_zero_zone_flag.py` still 3 of 3 (OPEN-66 core/perim cases still reroute). (2) One new test in `tests/test_eu_floor_division_residual_2026_09_29.py`: the spike ring above comes out with the spike removed and no two consecutive vertices within 0.01 m; one new test that a dwelling-only zone list whose `intersect_match` raises reaches `idf.match()` (monkeypatch). (3) OK-100: `at_risk` False and zone count equal to `ok100c` on 100 of 100, or each exception named. (4) 92: recovered N of 92 per district; every IDF in `u1g` scanned with EnergyPlus's own rule (drop a vertex collinear with its neighbours within 0.01 m, then count consecutive duplicates): 0 surfaces left with a doubled vertex. (5) `scratch/u1g_ep_list.txt` = every recovered IDF of `u1g`; stop, the director runs the proof on Speed. (6) The four T04f test files plus `test_builder_zero_zone_flag.py` green. Register the spike fatal and the guard cause in the debug references (rewrite the `[OPEN]` OK-100 entry, drop `[OPEN]`). Append a one-line director note under OPEN-66 T04 in the openings plan is **not** part of this task. **Then → T05 per CP-1c.**

- New branch in `build_merged_manifest` taken only when `merged_tag == "2026-09-29"`: base rows = `<D>_merged_2026-09-08/<slug>_manifest.csv` (all rows, order kept); when not `dry_run`, call `harvest_district(district, work_base, tag=FIX_TAG)` first; `fix_ok = _rc_zero_index(_load_manifest(district, FIX_TAG))`. Per base row: id in `fix_ok` → every `MANIFEST_COLUMNS` value from the fix row, `eui_source = FIX_TAG`; id in the fix `prepared_buildings.csv` but not in `fix_ok` → base row unchanged, `eui_source = "fix_failed_carry"`; otherwise base row unchanged, `eui_source = "merged_2026-09-08_carry"`. Keep the base row's own `eui_source` in a new column `base_eui_source`. The base `pending_resimulation` rows stay pending. `counts` keys: `FIX_TAG`, `fix_failed_carry`, `merged_2026-09-08_carry`, `pending_resimulation`.
- `build_summary` for this tag adds `fix_failed_ids` (sorted list) and `ceiling82_*` keys stay as they are. The 2026-09-07 and 2026-09-08 paths must give byte-identical output to today.
- Test: `--dry-run --merged-tag 2026-09-29` for all four districts prints counts whose sum equals the `_merged_2026-09-08` row count (Madrid 1187, Lyon 529, London 1240, Bologna 1215); `--dry-run --merged-tag 2026-09-08` counts unchanged from before the edit. No code comments; do not write any `_merged_*` folder; do not run the non-dry harvest.

### T06c — Surfaces between flats never face outside (`D-EU-133`, director, CP-3 ruling)

**What.** Four items, in order. Code changes in `scripts/run_eu_s2_campaign.py` only (`surfaces.py`, `european_nocore.py`, `european_residential.py` not edited).
1. **Scan (read-only, no rebuild).** One function `hidden_outdoor_area(idf_path)` in `run_eu_s2_campaign.py`: for every `BuildingSurface:Detailed` whose `Outside_Boundary_Condition` is `Outdoors` or `Ground`, the area (shapely, surface's own plane frame `_plane_frame`, as `_pair_covers_same_polygon` does) overlapped by any surface of **another zone** that lies within 0.01 m of its plane and faces the other way (normals dot < −0.99). Returns per building: `hidden_outdoor_m2`, `hidden_ground_m2`, surface count, and the envelope totals `outdoor_wall_m2`, `roof_m2`, `ground_floor_m2`, `outdoor_floor_m2`. Run it over (a) the 286 IDFs of `EU-11/<D>_fix_2026-09-29/idfs/` and their baseline IDFs (T05 `scratch/t05/base_idfs.csv`), (b) every one of the 3,874 non-candidate IDFs in the `_recut_2026-09-08` trees (the published 09-08 fleet). Output `division_residual_2026-09-29/hidden_outdoor_scan.csv`, one row per (district, building_id, tree). Expected on `way/311159684` (`dfd3c412acf87ad5`): outdoor walls 1,281.5 m² in the fix IDF vs about 450 m² in the baseline, most of the difference hidden.
2. **Cause per home.** For each fix-tree home with `hidden_outdoor_m2 + hidden_ground_m2 > 1.0` m², name the step that leaves the overlap unpaired, one token each: `intersect_raised_match_only` (`intersect_match` raised and the `match()`-alone retry ran), `repair_outdoors_remainder` (T04f `_split_larger_group` / `_nothing_across` turned a piece Outdoors that has a surface across it), `match_partial_overlap` (`intersect_match` ran but the two sides were never cut to the same polygon), `other` (quote what). Column `cause` in the scan CSV.
3. **Fix.** (a) Correct the step named in item 2 when it is `repair_outdoors_remainder` (the test "nothing across" must see every other-zone surface, paired or not). (b) New final pass `_pair_hidden_outdoor_surfaces(idf)`, called in `build_idf_for_building` after every existing repair and before the last `_clean_surface_rings` (T04h): for each `Outdoors`/`Ground` surface A overlapped by an opposite-facing coplanar surface B of another zone, split A and B into the overlap piece and their remainders (same eppy + shapely machinery as `_split_larger_group`, suffix `_H<n>`); the two overlap pieces become `Surface` pairs by exact mirror (`D-EU-118`); remainders keep their own condition. Fenestration on an overlap piece is deleted (a window between two flats is not a window), counted and its area reported. Where a piece has a hole or the split fails, the overlap piece becomes `Adiabatic`, **never** `Outdoors`/`Ground`. No new area cap; `MAX_ADIABATIC_REMAINDER_M2` does not apply to this pass. (c) Same run: `hidden_outdoor_area` of every IDF the pass touched is 0.0 (≤ 0.01 m²).
4. **Rebuild.** The 297 ids of `fix_candidates.csv` with the fixed code into `EU-11/<D>_fix_2026-09-30/` (`prepare --only-ids`, same files as the `_fix_2026-09-29` trees), four prepares at once, at most **5** local processes. `fleet.lst` of each tree lists only the ids whose `idf_sha256` differs from the `_fix_2026-09-29` IDF and that are not among the 11 dropped at CP-2; the ids with an unchanged IDF keep their T06 result.
**Why.** T06 §8, FINDING 277: 220 of 279 rebuilt homes carry walls and floors between flats as outside surfaces (outdoor-wall area median ×1.56 at an unchanged footprint, EUI ×1.7–1.9). Owner ruling at CP-3, 2026-09-30, verbatim: "i recommend yes" (to: keep the 09-08 EUIs, fix those walls, re-run the 220 on Speed). The class census and the crash proof are blind to boundary conditions, so the scan of item 1 becomes a gate.
**How.** No EnergyPlus, no cluster. No frozen rule changes (dwelling count, `k`, every check threshold, `MAX_FLAT_ASPECT`, `MIN_SPREAD`). Reuse `scratch/t05_build.py` for the rebuild.
**How to test.** (1) Unit tests appended to `tests/test_eu_floor_division_residual_2026_09_29.py`: two boxes side by side, the shared wall on one side `Outdoors` → paired as `Surface` by exact mirror, remainder stays `Outdoors`; the same with a window on the shared wall → window deleted; a ceiling with a floor above only on half of it → half paired, half kept as is; a wall with no surface across → unchanged. (2) Scan table: per district, homes with hidden area > 1.0 m², N of M, for the `_fix_2026-09-29` tree, its baseline, and the 3,874 non-candidates; causes counted. (3) Envelope gate on every rebuilt candidate, new IDF vs baseline IDF: `hidden_outdoor_m2 + hidden_ground_m2` ≤ 0.01 m² on N of 297; `outdoor_wall_m2`, `roof_m2`, `ground_floor_m2` each within 1 % of the baseline on N of 297 (the footprint and height did not change); every exception listed with its measured values. Window area deleted per district. (4) Before-class → after-class unchanged against T05 on N of 297 (the fix touches boundaries, not flats); zone count unchanged. (5) The T04h test list green. (6) Speed case count per district = lines of each `fleet.lst`. Register every error met in the debug references, and FINDING 277's cause in house format. **STOP → director ships on Speed.**

### T06e — Flats on one floor never overlap; repair the published homes too (`D-EU-134`, `FINDING 278`, director 2026-10-01)

**Why.** T06c gate (3b) failed on 240 of 297. Director measurement, 2026-10-01 (`division_residual_2026-09-29/zone_overlap_scan_director_2026-10-01.csv`: per IDF, sum over storeys of each zone's floor area vs the union of all zones' floors): flats on the same storey **overlap each other**. `way/435638870` (`d48b6476e707231d`): 5 ground-floor flats, floors sum 859.4 m² over a 284.6 m² footprint, every pair of flats shares 57–119 m²; the 09-08 IDF is one zone per floor, 284.6 m². Homes with overlap > 1 %: `fix_2026-09-30` 167 of 297 (Madrid 63/102, Lyon 21/27, London 12/51, Bologna 71/117; same in `fix_2026-09-29`); **published 09-08 fleet 85 of 4,171** (Madrid 31, Lyon 5, London 2, Bologna 47; extra floor area +3.0 / +1.4 / +1.3 / +6.1 % per district; 44 `DWELLING_LAYOUT_EMITTED_IMPUTED_COUNT`, 24 `DWELLING_LAYOUT_EMITTED`, 10 + 7 best effort; 6 of the 85 are candidates). The EUI denominator is `prepared_buildings.floor_area_m2` (footprint × storeys), so overlap inflates loads, not the denominator: the 85 published EUIs are biased upward. T06c also found hidden outdoor area on 1,160 of 3,874 published non-candidates. The 629 / 282 / 173 / 900 pieces T06c made `Adiabatic` (110,336 m²) are expected to be mostly these overlaps. Standing owner ruling (§7): most accurate route, so the published homes are rebuilt too.

**What.** Code in `scripts/run_eu_s2_campaign.py`; the file that holds the step named in item 2 may also be edited, for that step only. Frozen rules unchanged (dwelling count, `k`, check thresholds, `MAX_FLAT_ASPECT`, `MIN_SPREAD`).
1. **Census (read-only).** `zone_floor_overlap(idf_path)` in `run_eu_s2_campaign.py`: per storey (floor-surface z rounded 0.1 m), `sum_m2` = Σ over zones of the union of that zone's floor polygons, `union_m2` = union over all zones; returns per IDF `floor_sum_m2`, `floor_union_m2`, `overlap_pairs` (zone pairs sharing > 0.1 m²). Must reproduce the director CSV row for `d48b6476e707231d` (859.4 / 284.6 per storey 0). Run on `_recut_2026-09-08` (4,171), `_fix_2026-09-29` and `_fix_2026-09-30` (297 each). Output `division_residual_2026-09-29/zone_overlap_scan.csv`.
2. **Cause per home.** For each home with `floor_sum_m2 > 1.01 × floor_union_m2`, name the step whose output first holds two zones covering the same area (instrument the dwelling polygons after each step in a scratch run, not in the shipped code): one token each, quoting `file:line` — e.g. the cutter, the imputed-count path, a T04d/T04e/T04f/T04g repair that copies a piece instead of moving it, `other` (quote what). Column `overlap_cause`.
3. **Fix.** (a) Correct each named step so dwelling floors on a storey are disjoint and their union is the storey footprint. (b) Hard check in `build_idf_for_building`, after every repair: if any storey has `sum_m2 > 1.01 × union_m2`, the home is not emitted with flats; it falls back to one zone per floor (`geometry_outcome` suffix `_OVERLAP_FALLBACK`), never emitted overlapping. (c) Unit tests appended to `tests/test_eu_floor_division_residual_2026_09_29.py`: two flats sharing area → check fires and fallback emitted; disjoint flats → unchanged; one test per cause token fixed in (a).
4. **Trial (then STOP).** Rebuild 20 homes into `EU-11/<D>_fix_2026-10-01/` (`prepare --only-ids`, ≤ 5 local processes): the 5 worst overlap homes of the fix trees, the 5 worst of the published 85, 5 published non-candidates with the largest hidden outdoor area, 5 random candidates. Gate below on the 20. **STOP → director audits.**
5. **Rebuild (after director go).** Scope = union of the 297 candidates, the 85 published overlap homes, the 1,160 published hidden-outdoor homes, minus the 11 dropped at CP-2; into `EU-11/<D>_fix_2026-10-01/`, four prepares at once, ≤ 5 local processes. `fleet.lst` = ids whose `idf_sha256` differs from the `_recut_2026-09-08` IDF. *Amended 2026-10-01 (owner: "run every test, simulations on speed in parallel, i do not want to wait, hasten the process"):* scope = every home (owner OK, see 3(d) ruling); up to 10 local processes in total (Speed has no OpenUBEM code or environment, so the IDF build stays local); tests with `pytest -n 8`; **stream**: as soon as one district is built and its gate lines are written, write its `fleet.lst` (sorted longest first by the 09-08 run time, else floor area) and report that district to the director by a line in §8 (`#### T06e item 5 — <D> ready`) while the other districts keep building.

*Director ruling 2026-10-01 (after the items 1–2 STOP, `surfaces.py` stays frozen, `D-EU-41`):* item 3(a) is done as option (a): new step `_clip_zone_horizontals_to_own_polygon(idf, dwelling_polygons)` in `run_eu_s2_campaign.py`, called in `build_idf_for_building` right after the extrusion/`intersect_match` stage and before every existing repair. For each `Floor` / `Ceiling` / `Roof` surface, clip it (shapely, plan view) to its own zone's dwelling polygon on that storey; drop pieces with < 0.01 m² left; keep the outer ring orientation and z. Then the floor/ceiling pieces between storeys are re-paired by the existing mirror repair (`D-EU-118`) and the T06c pass; any piece that stays unpaired with a surface across it is `Adiabatic`, never `Outdoors`/`Ground`. 3(b) fallback stays as the safety net and must fire on 0 of the trial homes that the clip fixes. Unit tests per 3(c), plus: two flats on a storey whose floor pieces overlap after a mock `intersect_match` → clipped to disjoint, union = footprint.

*Director ruling 2026-10-01, CP-3a (`D-EU-135`, `FINDING 279`), after the items 3–4 STOP.* Audit: G1 20/20, G2 20/20, G3 13/20, G4 13/20, fallback 0/20, tests 124/124 (recounted from `t06e_gate.csv`); the district-campaign 3-line edit is accepted (it is item 3(b)). The finding is confirmed and is wider than the trial. `_extrude_zone_groups` (`openubem/idf/surfaces.py:820-869`) groups zones by `(prefix, label)` (`_group_key`, `:122`) and extrudes `group[0]["coords_m"]` through `n = len(group)` storeys from z = 0, so every storey gets the storey-0 outline of each label, and a label missing on some storeys is shifted down (enumerate index, not its `F` index). Director scan of the 09-08 layouts (`layouts/**/*.json`, divided homes only, outcome without `REROUTED`/`ONE_ZONE`): a flat whose outline differs between storeys by > 1 % (all of them also > 10 %) in **Madrid 530 of 1,079, Lyon 192 of 471, London 130 of 1,190, Bologna 921 of 1,078**; the flat area placed on the wrong outline is 21.2 / 19.3 / 14.3 / 30.5 % of the flat area. Example Bologna `87532` (`ff2ed0a61407c7c9`): storeys 0–3 hold 3 flats, storeys 4–5 hold 2 flats of other outlines; the IDF stacks the storey-0 outlines of labels 0 and 1 to storey 5. So the published IDFs of about 1,770 homes do not follow their own layouts; the 85 overlap homes are the visible part. Ruling:
- **3(d) Per-storey extrusion, campaign-local.** New `_extrude_zone_groups_per_storey(idf, valid_zones, _exc)` in `run_eu_s2_campaign.py`. It groups by `(prefix, label)` as before, then splits each group into runs of consecutive `F` indices with the same outline (symmetric difference ≤ 0.01 m²). A run equal to the whole group starting at F0 makes exactly the frozen call (`add_block(coords, n*h, n)`, same names, same bbox fallback), so a home whose outlines never change gets the same IDF as before. Any other run of `m` storeys starting at `F a` is `add_block(coords, m*h, m)` with every new surface moved up by `a*h`. Its bottom floor at z > 0 must never be `Ground`: set it to `Outdoors` before `intersect_match`, which then pairs it, and any piece left uncovered goes to the clip/hidden passes. Zones keep their `F` index z. `build_idf_for_building` installs it as `openubem.idf.surfaces._extrude_zone_groups` for that one call, so the retry at `:945` uses it too, and restores the original in `finally`. `surfaces.py` is not edited (`D-EU-41`), and no other caller (the openings/Canada path) sees the change.
- The clip (3(a)) stays as the safety net after the new extrusion, and the 3(b) fallback stays.
- **Tests (3(c) plus):** (i) a constant-outline group gives byte-identical surfaces to the frozen function; (ii) storeys 0–3 with 3 flats and storeys 4–5 with 2 other flats give per-zone floor = own layout polygon, no `Ground` above z = 0, correct z per `F` index; (iii) a label present on F2–F3 only sits at 2h–4h; (iv) after the build, `surfaces._extrude_zone_groups` is the original object.
- **Item 4b re-trial (then STOP → CP-3a again).** The same 20 homes plus 10 published, divided non-candidates from the director scan (5 Bologna, 2 Madrid, 2 Lyon, 1 London, seed 20261001, stacking-affected), rebuilt into `EU-11/<D>_fix_2026-10-01/` (overwrite the trial). Gate G1–G10 on the 30.
- **Item 5 scope changes.** Rebuild every home of `_recut_2026-09-08` (4,171) except the 11 dropped at CP-2, so that the whole published set follows one code. `fleet.lst` = ids whose `idf_sha256` differs from the 09-08 IDF (expected about 1,800 to 2,100).
- **Item 5b, plan pages for the owner (owner request 2026-10-01: "before simulations i would like to see our plan division results in plans3D"), then STOP.** After the item-5 gate passes, run `python scripts/eu18_emit_plan_pages.py` with one `--evidence-root <D>=openubem/outputs/eu_evidence/EU-11/<D>_fix_2026-10-01` per district. It reads the new IDFs, so the pages show what will be simulated. Rename each output to `PLANS_<D>_2026-10-01.html` and move the four `*_nocore_2026-09-07_r6.html` files into `plans3D/archive/`. Then make every link in `plans3D/index.html` point to the new pages (grep shows 0 links to r6 and 4 to 2026-10-01) and sweep the citations to the r6 pages (`.md`, `.py`, `.html`). *Amended 2026-10-01 (owner "i do not want to wait"):* the pages are made per district as soon as that district is ready, **in parallel with** its Speed run. Speed does not wait for the page review; if the owner sees a fault, the director cancels the affected arrays.
- *Director audit of 4b, 2026-10-01 (CP-3a passed; go for item 5).* The counts were redone from `t06e_gate.csv`: G1 30/30, G2 30/30, G3 30/30, G9 29/30, G4 14/30, G10 0/1. Fallback 0/30, tests 128/128, `surfaces.py` unchanged since 09-30 00:02. Rulings for item 5:
  - **G4** gates only homes whose outlines never change. On every other home the 09-08 walls followed the stacked storey-0 outline, so G4 is reported there but does not gate.
  - **G9** exceptions are allowed if the zones of each storey still add up to the layout's storey area within 1 %. Such homes are listed, and `31269` is one of them. Any other G9 exception is a STOP.
  - **G10** is measured on the full rebuild. Below 95 % of the in-scope homes is a STOP.
  - The 4 failing tests in `test_eu_s2_campaign_ceiling82.py` already failed before 3(d). They stay [OPEN] and do not block item 5.
- **Item 5 dispatch (director).** One executor per district runs in parallel: Bologna 3 processes, Madrid 3, Lyon 2, London 2, so 10 in total.
  - Each executor builds its own district and computes the gate on it. It writes `division_residual_2026-09-29/t06e_gate_<D>.csv` and `EU-11/<D>_fix_2026-10-01/fleet.lst`. The list holds the stems whose `idf_sha256` differs from 09-08, longest first (by 09-08 run time where known, else floor area).
  - It then runs item 5b for its own district with `--district <D>`. `index.html` is fixed by the director at the end.
  - It appends its §8 entry with a single shell `cat >>` (four writers share this file) and stops.
  - The director submits each district to Speed as soon as it is reported.
- **Gate changes.** G3 is compared to the layout, not to the 09-08 IDF. The ground floor = the storey-0 layout union within 1 %. The roof projection = the union of all storeys' layout outlines within 1 %. New gates:
  - (G9) Every zone's floor area is within max(1 %, 0.05 m²) of its own layout polygon. Gate line = homes with all zones passing, worst 10 listed.
  - (G10) On homes with constant outlines and no 09-08 overlap or hidden area, the new `idf_sha256` = the 09-08 sha (N of M). Every exception is listed with its cause.

**Gate (every rebuilt home, new IDF vs `_recut_2026-09-08` IDF; N of M per line, every exception listed with measured values in `division_residual_2026-09-29/t06e_gate.csv`).**
- (G1) overlap: `floor_sum_m2 ≤ 1.01 × floor_union_m2`.
- (G2) hidden outdoor + ground ≤ 0.01 m².
- (G3) ground floor = storey-0 union of the 09-08 IDF within 1 %; roof (horizontal projection union) within 1 % of the 09-08 IDF.
- (G4) outdoor wall within 1 % of (09-08 outdoor wall − 09-08 hidden outdoor wall area).
- (G5) `Adiabatic` area made by `_pair_hidden_outdoor_surfaces` per district, before vs after (expected to fall); every home with > 5 m² listed.
- (G6) class table 09-08 → new per district; any move to a worse class or to `_OVERLAP_FALLBACK` listed; zone count reported.
- (G7) T04h list and the residual test file green.
- (G8) Speed case count per district = `fleet.lst` lines.
Register every error met and `FINDING 278` (cause + fix) in the debug references, house format.

**How.** No EnergyPlus, no cluster. Reuse the T06c scratch scripts and `scratch/t05_build.py`.

**Amends T06d.** Harvest tag becomes `FIX3_TAG = "fix_2026-10-01"`, `merged_tag == "2026-10-01"`, base rows = `<D>_merged_2026-09-08/<slug>_manifest.csv` (the adopted set; 09-29 and 09-30 were never adopted), carry `eui_source = "merged_2026-09-08_carry"` / `fix3_failed_carry`. `_fix_2026-09-30` is not shipped.

### T06f — No degenerate surface reaches EnergyPlus (`D-EU-137`, director 2026-10-01, after the first London failures)

**Why.** London job `1405523`: 9 of the first 38 finished tasks are `FAILED` in 1–5 s at `GetSurfaceData`, two signatures, both on pieces made by the T06e per-storey extrusion + clip (names end `_K1_K1`, `_K1_H1`): (a) `** Severe ** CheckConvexity: Surface="BLOCK 33F000A6E847D20C_2_A0 STOREY 1 ROOF 0001_K1_K1" is non-planar` then `GetSurfaceData: Zero or negative surface area[2.37637E-010]` (3 collinear vertices); (b) `RoofCeiling:Detailed="BLOCK 4A18CE956D10790E_3_A2 STOREY 0 FLOOR 0001_K1_H1", Vertex size mismatch ... The vertex sizes are 3 for base surface and 4 for outside boundary surface` (a 2 cm × 1.5 m four-vertex piece with two vertices 3 mm apart; EnergyPlus merges them on one side only). Director scan of the four shipped `fleet.lst` (scratch `sliver.py`: area < 1e-4 m² = SLIVER; area < 0.05 m² or two consecutive vertices < 1 cm apart = THIN): homes flagged London 88/191 (SLIVER 23), Lyon 79/249 (7), Madrid 198/747 (12), Bologna 275/1,089 (11). On London's 38 finished tasks: every failure is flagged; 15 of 15 unflagged homes completed; 14 flagged homes also completed. The T02b `_drop_sliver_surfaces` (`run_eu_s2_campaign.py`, after the mirror) did not catch these pieces (find out why: order of calls vs the clip, the `max_total_m2 = 0.1` all-or-nothing cap, or the 1e-2 threshold).

**What.** Code in `scripts/run_eu_s2_campaign.py` only (`surfaces.py` frozen, `D-EU-41`; frozen rules unchanged).
1. `ep_geometry_preflight(idf) -> list[tuple[str, str]]` returns `(surface_name, token)` for: `ZERO_AREA` (Newell area < 1e-4 m², or fewer than 3 vertices after merging consecutive vertices closer than 0.01 m); `VERTEX_MISMATCH` (an interzone pair whose vertex counts differ after merging vertices < 0.01 m apart and removing collinear vertices, sin angle < 1e-4); `NO_PARTNER` (outside-boundary object missing). It must flag all 9 failed London stems on their shipped IDF (stems from `sacct -j 1405523 -X -s F` mapped through `fleet.lst`; the director lists them: `33f000a6e847d20c`, `d34804e7432973aa`, `ffe50662baac87f8`, `9e3b369affce3b10`, `c98222345576c126`, `4a18ce956d10790e`, `2fdb085072a87fff` + any new ones in the London `FAILED` set at run time).
2. Fix, as the last geometry step of `build_idf_for_building` (after the clip, mirror, T06c pass and `_drop_sliver_surfaces`): on every horizontal surface, merge consecutive vertices < 0.01 m apart and remove collinear vertices; then mirror the cleaned ring onto its interzone partner so both sides have the same vertex list reversed; then drop any surface that is left with < 3 vertices or area < 0.01 m², together with its partner(s) and hosted fenestration. No all-or-nothing cap; log `degenerate_removed`, `degenerate_area_m2` per home. Then `ep_geometry_preflight` must return []; if it does not, the home is not emitted with flats and falls back to one zone per floor (`_PREFLIGHT_FALLBACK` suffix), never emitted failing.
3. Unit tests appended to `tests/test_eu_floor_division_residual_2026_09_29.py`: collinear 3-vertex roof dropped; 4-vertex piece with 3 mm vertex pair → both sides 3 vertices or dropped as a pair; a clean pair is byte-identical; preflight on a clean IDF returns [].

**How to test / rebuild (stream per district, ≤ 10 local processes, `pytest -n 8`, no EnergyPlus).** Rebuild every stem of the four `EU-11/<D>_fix_2026-10-01/fleet.lst` into `EU-11/<D>_fix_2026-10-01_t06f/` (same layout: `idfs/`, `schedules/`, manifest). Gate per district, N of M, written to `division_residual_2026-09-29/t06f_gate_<D>.csv`:
- (F1) `ep_geometry_preflight` = [] on M of M rebuilt IDFs; and it flags every listed failed London stem on its `_fix_2026-10-01` IDF.
- (F2) per home, Σ floor area within max(0.1 %, 0.5 m²) of the `_fix_2026-10-01` IDF; zone count equal; `_PREFLIGHT_FALLBACK` count (expected 0, each listed).
- (F3) homes the director scan did not flag: new `idf_sha256` = `_fix_2026-10-01` sha (N of M, exceptions listed with cause).
- (F4) T04h list and the residual test file green.
Write `EU-11/<D>_fix_2026-10-01_t06f/changed.lst` = stems whose sha differs from `_fix_2026-10-01`. Append one §8 line per district `#### T06f — <D> ready` with a single shell `cat >>`, then STOP after all four. Register both EnergyPlus signatures (cause + fix) in the debug references, extending the `D-EU-43` / sliver entries, not duplicating them.

**Director after T06f.** Copy changed IDFs over the Speed `idfs/` with tar to a temp folder then `mv` (atomic, the arrays are live and read `idfs/<stem>.idf` at task start); resubmit only the `FAILED` indices of the four jobs with `--array=<list>%32`. Tasks already `COMPLETED` on the old IDF keep their result (the removed pieces are < 0.01 m² each; logged as a deviation).

### T06d2 — Harvest code for the `fix_2026-10-01` fleet (director 2026-10-01; replaces T06d + its T06e amendment)

**Why.** The four `fix_2026-10-01` arrays run on Speed with a mix of IDFs: the stems in `EU-11/<D>_fix_2026-10-01/shipped_t06f.lst` (director-written, Lyon 66 / London 23 / Madrid 135 / Bologna 295; the director appends to it at each later swap) ran `EU-11/<D>_fix_2026-10-01_t06f/idfs/<stem>.idf`; every other `fleet.lst` stem ran `EU-11/<D>_fix_2026-10-01/idfs/<stem>.idf`. Failed tasks are resubmitted as new array jobs on the same `fleet.lst` index (1405950 Lyon, 1405951 London, 1405952 Madrid so far). The manifest must name the IDF that was actually simulated, and the merged set must carry 09-08 rows for every home not in `fleet.lst`.

**What.** Code in `scripts/cluster/harvest_eu11_district.py` and `scripts/cluster/harvest_eu11_merged.py` only.
1. `harvest_eu11_district.py`: `TAGGED_DISTRICT_JOBS` values may be an `int` or a list of ints; add `fix_2026-10-01` = Madrid `[1405556, 1405952]`, Lyon `[1405481, 1405950]`, London `[1405523, 1405951]`, Bologna `[1405559]`. `run_seconds` per index comes from the last job in the list that has that index `COMPLETED` (else the last that has it at all); `summary.json` `speed_job_id` becomes the list. Existing int entries behave exactly as before.
2. Same file, `harvest_district`: if `<dist_dir>/shipped_t06f.lst` exists, for each listed stem the manifest `idf_sha256` = sha256 of `<district>_<tag>_t06f/idfs/<stem>.idf` (bytes), else `prepared["idf_sha256"]`; add a column `idf_variant` (`t06f` / `fix`) at the end of the row (not in `MANIFEST_COLUMNS`). A stem in `shipped_t06f.lst` but not in `fleet.lst` raises.
3. `harvest_eu11_merged.py`: `FIX3_TAG = "fix_2026-10-01"`; `merged_tag == "2026-10-01"` → `_build_fix3_manifest(district, dry_run)`, a copy of `_build_fix_manifest` except: base rows = `<D>_merged_2026-09-08/<slug>_manifest.csv`; fix manifest = `FIX3_TAG`; `fix_prepared_ids` = the building ids of the `fleet.lst` stems (map stem → building_id through `<D>_fix_2026-10-01/prepared_buildings.csv`), not every prepared id; sources `FIX3_TAG` / `fix3_failed_carry` / `merged_2026-09-08_carry` / `pending_resimulation`; carry `idf_variant` (NA on carried rows). Output folder `_merged_2026-10-01` exactly as the `2026-09-29` branch writes `_merged_2026-09-29` (same summary fields). The 5 held-back homes (`FINDING 280`) and the 11 dropped at CP-2 are not in `fleet.lst`, so they fall to `merged_2026-09-08_carry` with no special code; the summary lists the 5 by building id under `held_back_finding280`.
4. Unit tests in `tests/test_harvest_eu11_fix3_2026_10_01.py` (no network; monkeypatch `fetch_district_out` / `fetch_sacct_elapsed` / `_ssh`): list-of-jobs elapsed override; sha from `_t06f` IDF for a listed stem; unlisted stem keeps prepared sha; stem in shipped list but not in `fleet.lst` raises; fix3 branch source counts on a 6-row toy base (1 fix ok, 1 fix failed, 1 not in fleet, 1 pending).

**How to test.** `pytest tests/test_harvest_eu11_fix3_2026_10_01.py` green; existing harvest tests green (`pytest -k harvest -n 8`). `python scripts/cluster/harvest_eu11_merged.py --district <D> --dry-run --merged-tag 2026-10-01`, once per district (no network, no folder written) gives row sums 1187 / 529 / 1240 / 1215 and, before the jobs finish, `fix3_failed_carry` = `fleet.lst` count (747 / 249 / 191 / 1,089); `--dry-run --merged-tag 2026-09-29` and `2026-09-08` counts unchanged from before the edit. No non-dry harvest, no ssh, no `_merged_2026-10-01` folder written. Append `#### T06d2 — harvest code — completed <date>` to §8 and STOP.

### T06d3 — Re-simulated pending homes must take their new result (director audit of T06d2, 2026-10-01)

**Why.** Director measured: every 09-08 `pending_resimulation` home is in `fleet.lst` (Madrid 17/17, Lyon 2/2, London 0/0, Bologna 10/10 = 29). `_build_fix3_manifest` tests `base_source == "pending_resimulation"` before `building_id in fix_ok`, so these 29 homes stay pending even when their new run completes — coverage silently lost.

### T06d4 — Early EUI check on the homes already finished (director 2026-10-01; CP-3b criterion run early)

**Why.** CP-3b adopts the new set only if every per-home EUI ratio outside 0.8–1.25 vs 09-08 is explained. ~600 homes have finished (London 168, Madrid 214+, Lyon 80+); a systematic error (like the 2× heating of the `_fix_2026-09-29` set) must be caught now, not after ~20 h of Speed time. The same comparison is re-run at CP-3b on the merged set.

**What.**
1. New script `scripts/cluster/compare_eu11_eui_ratio.py`: args `--district <D>` and `--manifest <path>` (any manifest with `MANIFEST_COLUMNS`, optional `idf_variant`). Loads base `EU-11/<D>_merged_2026-09-08/<slug>_manifest.csv`; keeps rows of the given manifest with `eplus_return_code == 0`; joins on `building_id` with base rows that have a numeric `eui_kwh_m2`. Per home: `eui_ratio`, `heating_ratio`, `floor_area_ratio` (new / 09-08), `geometry_outcome` new and old, `idf_variant`, `flag_out = eui_ratio < 0.8 or > 1.25`. Writes `<out-prefix>.csv` (one row per home) and `<out-prefix>_summary.json`: n joined; median, p05, p95 of the three ratios; count `flag_out`; of the flagged, how many have `floor_area_ratio` outside 0.98–1.02 (floor changed) vs heating-only; same split by `idf_variant`; pooled EUI new vs pooled 09-08 EUI on the same homes (sum heating / sum floor). `--out-prefix` default `EU-11/division_residual_2026-09-29/eui_ratio_<slug>_early_2026-10-01`.
2. Run `python scripts/cluster/harvest_eu11_district.py --district <D> --tag fix_2026-10-01` for GB-LDN-STDUNSTANS, ES-MAD-BERRUGUETE, FR-LYO-HAUTCOEURPENTES (not Bologna, none finished). It fetches only finished outputs over ssh (`tar` on the login node, allowed) and writes `<D>_fix_2026-10-01/<slug>_manifest.csv`; this partial manifest is overwritten by the final harvest. Then delete the local work folder `%TEMP%/ubem_eu11_harvest/<D>_fix_2026-10-01` by that exact name so the final harvest starts clean. Then run the script of item 1 on each partial manifest.
3. Tests `tests/test_compare_eu11_eui_ratio.py` on a toy pair of manifests (ratio math, rc filter, flag thresholds, floor vs heating split).

**How to test.** New tests green. Report per district: n joined, median eui_ratio, count outside 0.8–1.25, of which floor-changed vs heating-only, pooled EUI new vs 09-08 on the same homes. No new submissions, no `scancel`, no merged harvest. Append `#### T06d4 — early EUI check — completed <date>` and STOP.

### T06g — Floors between storeys never face outside (`D-EU-138`, `FINDING 281`, director 2026-10-01 after the T06d4 audit)

**Why.** T06d4 flagged `way/1237260791` (`a2c94661a5028bb1`, Madrid) at heating 1.66× its 09-08 value. In its `_fix_2026-10-01` IDF every storey F0–F5 has 2 `Roof` pieces (`Outdoors`) at its ceiling height and the storey above has 2 `Floor` pieces (`Outdoors`) at the same height, same outline (e.g. `F0_dwelling_2` "Storey 0 Ceiling 0001_3" and `F1_dwelling_2` "Storey 1 Floor 0001_4", 154.5 m² each, identical first vertices). Their vertex order is wrong on one side of each pair (`F0_dwelling_3` roof normal points **down**, `F1_dwelling_2` floor normal points **up**), so the two sides face the same way: `intersect_match` does not pair them and `_hidden_pairs` (`run_eu_s2_campaign.py:831`, test `normals @ normals[i] < HIDDEN_FACING_DOT`) skips them. EnergyPlus then loses heat to outside air through 3,709 m² of inner floors. Director scan (outdoor horizontal surface overlapping > 0.1 m² another zone's horizontal surface at the same z, any orientation; list `division_residual_2026-09-29/t06g_stems_director_2026-10-01.csv`): **40 homes** in the shipped trees (`_fix_2026-10-01` and `_t06f` give the same 40): Madrid 9, Lyon 2, London 1, Bologna 28; 38 of 40 had 0 m² of it in their 09-08 IDF (12 were one zone per floor at 09-08 after an interzone mismatch). Every per-storey union is unchanged (symmetric difference 0.0 m² on `a2c94661a5028bb1`), so the area is right and only the orientation is wrong.

**What.** Code in `scripts/run_eu_s2_campaign.py` only (`surfaces.py` frozen, `D-EU-41`).
1. Find where the wrong vertex order is made (candidates: the move-up of runs in `_extrude_zone_groups_per_storey` `:1080`, the clip, `_clean_surface_rings`, the T06f `_remove_degenerate_surfaces`); name function:line in §8.
2. Fix it at that step. Then add a guard, run in `build_idf_for_building` **before** `_pair_hidden_outdoor_surfaces`: every horizontal surface (all vertex z within 0.01 m) of type `Floor` gets Newell normal z < 0, of type `Roof`/`Ceiling` z > 0; a wrong one has its vertex order reversed (count `orientation_fixed` per home). The T06c pass then pairs the facing pieces as usual.
3. Add token `EXPOSED_BETWEEN_ZONES` to `ep_geometry_preflight`: an `Outdoors` or `Ground` horizontal surface whose plan polygon overlaps by > 0.1 m² a horizontal surface of another zone at the same z (within 0.01 m), any orientation. A home that still carries it falls back as T06f does (`_PREFLIGHT_FALLBACK`).
4. Tests appended to `tests/test_eu_floor_division_residual_2026_09_29.py`: a two-storey toy with one flipped roof and one flipped floor → after the build step both are `Surface` pairs or `Adiabatic`, never `Outdoors`; preflight flags the flipped toy before the fix and returns [] after; a clean toy is byte-identical.
5. Harvest: `scripts/cluster/harvest_eu11_district.py` reads an optional `shipped_t06g.lst` next to `shipped_t06f.lst`; a stem in it gets `idf_variant = t06g` (takes precedence over `t06f`); same raise rule for a stem not in `fleet.lst`; one test.

**How to test / rebuild (≤ 10 local processes, `pytest -n 8`, no EnergyPlus).** Rebuild the 40 stems into `EU-11/<D>_fix_2026-10-01_t06g/` (same layout as `_t06f`) and 40 control homes (10 per district, seed 20261001, from `fleet.lst` minus the 40). Gate written to `division_residual_2026-09-29/t06g_gate.csv`:
- (H1) `EXPOSED_BETWEEN_ZONES` and the whole preflight = [] on 40 of 40 rebuilt; the new token flags 40 of 40 on their `_t06f` (or `_fix_2026-10-01` if absent from `_t06f`) IDF.
- (H2) per home, Σ floor area within max(0.1 %, 0.5 m²) of the `_t06f` IDF; zone count equal; `_PREFLIGHT_FALLBACK` count (expected 0, each listed).
- (H3) 40 controls: `idf_sha256` equal to their `_t06f` IDF, N of 40, every exception named with cause.
- (H4) whole fleet: the new token over every `_t06f` IDF of the four `fleet.lst` → count of homes (expected exactly the 40; any other listed).
- (H5) residual test file, T04h list, harvest tests green.
Write `EU-11/<D>_fix_2026-10-01_t06g/changed.lst`. Register the cause in the debug references. Append `#### T06g — floors between storeys — completed <date>` with one shell `cat >>` and STOP.

**Director after T06g.** Copy the changed IDFs onto Speed `idfs/` (tar → temp → `mv`); for each of the 40: not started → nothing more; finished or failed → `rm -rf out/<stem>` and resubmit its index with `%32`; running → resubmit after it ends. Write `shipped_t06g.lst` per district.

### T06d5 — Every flagged home gets a named reason (director 2026-10-01; CP-3b criterion)

**Why.** T06d4: 227 of 626 finished homes are outside 0.8–1.25, all with floor area unchanged. Director join with `hidden_outdoor_scan.csv` (09-08 trees): per-home heating ratio vs (1 − hidden share of the 09-08 envelope) correlates 0.875 / 0.878 / 0.931 (Madrid / Lyon / London); homes with no hidden area keep heating ratio median 1.013 / 1.032 / 1.036; flagged homes with hidden share > 2 %: 92 of 95 / 53 of 57 / 73 of 75. The fall is the T06c correction (09-08 lost heat through walls that were inside the building). The remaining 9 are: a changed one-zone/divided outcome (Madrid 3, Lyon 1), a floor change (Lyon 1), stacked storey-0 outlines fixed (London `way/394093228` / `…227`: per-storey floor union +28 %, outdoor wall 22.8 → 68.5 m²; Lyon 2 at 1.25–1.27), and the T06g fault. CP-3b needs this on every home, by code.

**What.** Extend `scripts/cluster/compare_eu11_eui_ratio.py` (no other script): new column `reason` on every flagged row, first match wins:
1. `t06g_fault` — stem in `t06g_stems_director_2026-10-01.csv` and its `idf_variant` is not `t06g`;
2. `floor_changed` — `floor_area_ratio` outside 0.98–1.02;
3. `outcome_changed` — `geometry_outcome` new ≠ old;
4. `hidden_0908` — 09-08 hidden area (`hidden_outdoor_m2 + hidden_ground_m2` of the `noncandidate_2026-09-08` or `baseline_2026-09-08` row in `hidden_outdoor_scan.csv`) ≥ 2 % of its 09-08 envelope (`outdoor_wall_m2 + roof_m2 + ground_floor_m2 + outdoor_floor_m2`), and `heating_ratio < 1`;
5. `storey_outline` — `union_ratio_vs_0908` in `t06e_gate_<D>.csv` outside 0.98–1.02, or `G4` False with `constant_outline` False;
6. `unexplained`.
Also write `hidden_share_0908` and `union_ratio_vs_0908` on every row, and per-reason counts in the summary json. Add `--tag-label` so the CP-3b run can write `eui_ratio_<slug>_merged_2026-10-01`. Tests: one toy row per reason.

**How to test.** Tests green. Re-run on the three early manifests (same out-prefix, overwrite). Report per district the counts per reason and list every `unexplained` home with its ratios. Append `#### T06d5 — named reasons — completed <date>` and STOP.

### T06i — `plans3D/` pages in the r2 template, from the simulated IDFs (director 2026-10-01, owner)

**Why.** Owner, 2026-10-01, on the four `PLANS_<D>_2026-10-01.html` pages made by `scripts/eu18_emit_plan_pages.py`: "look how absurd the content for these compared the previous version file:///C:/Users/o_iseri/Desktop/OpenUBEM/docs/docs_ACTIVE/europeanLocations/plans3D/archive/PLANS_GB-LDN-STDUNSTANS_nocore_2026-09-03_r2.html i said to you i want t osee similar template for plans 3d like outputs 3d except EUI result filter like the previous ones". The r2 page (3D district scene, HUD, legend, click a building → floor-plan panel with a storey bar) is written by `build_html` in `scripts/eu21/08_district_viewer.py:326`, but that script re-runs the cutter instead of reading what is simulated. The new pages keep the r2 look and show the shipped division.

**What.** New script `scripts/eu21/09_plans3d_from_idf.py`; no edit to `08_district_viewer.py` or `eu18_emit_plan_pages.py`. It imports `build_html` and its helpers/constants from `08_district_viewer.py` with the `_load_module` pattern (`:29`). Source of every building: the IDF in `openubem/outputs/eu_evidence/EU-11/<D>_fix_2026-10-01_t06f/idfs/` when the stem is there (the file on Speed), else the IDF in `<D>_fix_2026-10-01/idfs/` (whole district: Madrid 1,183, Lyon 529, London 1,239, Bologna 1,209 — same population as the current pages). Read with `scripts.eu_idf_plan_reader` (`read_district` / `BuildingPlan`, as `eu18_emit_plan_pages.py:98-153` does; build a merged root or read twice and override by stem). Map each `BuildingPlan` to one `plates` record of the shape `build_html` reads (`:330-366`): `building_id`, `footprint` (local rings), `footprint_scene` (scene ring around the district centroid, as `main` does with `M3D._scene_ring`, `:873-876`; footprint from `EU02/<D>/02_residential_manifest.gpkg` as in `main`), `height_m`, `storeys`, `area_m2` (gross), `k` (dwellings per storey), `dwellings`, `status`/`verdict`, `group`, `idf_state` = `geometry_outcome`, `declared_dwellings` = `dwelling_count`. Per storey: the r2 panel has a storey bar (`:446`, `:509`, `:579`); carry the real zones of every storey (new per-storey field) and make the smallest JS change so the bar switches the drawn plate; replace the fixed note "the same plan on every storey (D-EU-79)" (`:447`, `:555`) with the true one for that building. Circulation zones in the circulation colour, dwellings in the dwelling colour, one-zone-per-floor homes labelled so. Checks `C1…C11`: if a function in `08_district_viewer.py` or the cutter evaluates them on given dwelling polygons, run it on the shipped zones of the typical storey; otherwise leave `ch` empty and say so in §8. Header line: "from the simulated IDFs (`<D>_fix_2026-10-01` + `_t06f`)". No EUI anywhere.

Four districts in parallel (4 local processes max). No EnergyPlus, nothing on Speed.

**How to test (P1–P4, N of M per district, into §8).** (P1) buildings on page = IDFs read = population above. (P2) every building, every storey: sum of drawn zone areas = IDF zone floor area within 0.5 % (scripted). (P3) parse each page's embedded scene JSON: parses, 0 keys containing `eui`, storey list length = IDF storey count for every building. (P4) for London `way/333138130` and 5 random homes per district print storeys, dwellings per storey, gross area from the page JSON next to the IDF values. Page size per district in MB. One §8 entry `#### T06i — r2-template plan pages — completed 2026-10-01`, plus a debug-ref entry for any error met.
