#### T06s — floor area kept through the flat-count rebuild (FINDING 289)

**What.** Part 1 (read-only, STOP after): for every IDF of `EU-11/<D>_win_2026-10-05/idfs/` and `<D>_win_2026-10-03/idfs/` (four districts), compute total zone floor area (sum of `Floor` surfaces per zone), number of zones, zones taller than 1.5 × storey height, floor surfaces per zone, and the storey count from zone `F` indices and from z levels. List every stem whose 10-05 total differs from 10-03 by more than 1 % → `division_residual_2026-09-29/t06s_floor_area_census.csv` (district, stem, building_id, area_03, area_05, ratio, storeys_03, storeys_05, zones_03, zones_05, dwellings old/new, tall zones 05, zero-floor zones 05). Then find the cause(s) in code with file:line for each class (down: one tall zone per dwelling with a single floor; up: not known — check for duplicated or overlapping floors on a storey with `zone_floor_overlap`; zero floor: `97d478bc56840531_F0_dwelling_3`). Report counts per class and per district.
**Why.** D-EU-141 changes how many flats a storey is cut into, never the building's floor area or storeys. A home whose zones lose or gain floor gets the wrong heated area in EnergyPlus (internal gains, ventilation and the EUI denominator all scale with it).
**How.** Scratch script outside the repo; reuse `zone_floor_overlap` (`run_eu_s2_campaign.py`) and the T06p build path (`build_idf_for_building`, `_extrude_zone_groups_per_storey`). No code edited, no EnergyPlus, no ssh.
**How to test.** Census totals reproduce the director counts (down > 5 %: ES 26, FR 6, GB 11, IT 148; up > 5 %: ES 15, FR 166); one worked example per class with the zone list of 10-03 and 10-05.
**Part 2 (after the director audit of part 1):** fix in the build path, rebuild only the affected stems into `<D>_win_2026-10-06` (= 10-05 with those stems replaced, same origin shift, same sliver step), gates: every stem's total zone floor area within 1 % of 10-03 or a named reason; zero-floor zones 0; all T06p/T06q gates re-run on the rebuilt stems; md5 list. Then Speed re-runs of the rebuilt stems only, harvest with the 10-06 tree, CP-3c.


#### T06s part 1 — floor-area census — completed 2026-10-02

- **Artifacts:** `openubem/outputs/eu_evidence/EU-11/division_residual_2026-09-29/t06s_floor_area_census.csv` (383 rows: every stem with a 10-05 vs 10-03 zone-floor total differing by more than 1 %, plus the zero-floor stem; columns of the task plus `storeys_*_F` (from zone `F` index) and `storeys_*_z` (from floor z levels), `floors_per_zone_max_05`, `cls`, `mech`). Scratch scripts only under the session scratchpad `t06s/` (`census.py`, `show.py`, `geom.py`, `zero.py`). No repo file edited, no EnergyPlus, no ssh. `tall_zones_05` = zones taller than 4.5 m (1.5 x 3.0 m storey).
- **Counts (4,113 IDF pairs, 0 missing; ratio = 10-05 / 10-03 total zone floor area, from the IDFs):**

| District | stems | down > 5 % | up > 5 % | zero-floor | down 1-5 % | up 1-5 % |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| ES-MAD | 1,172 | 26 | 16 | 0 | 0 | 0 |
| FR-LYO | 522 | 6 | 169 | 0 | 1 | 0 |
| GB-LDN | 1,240 | 11 | 0 | 0 | 1 | 0 |
| IT-BOL | 1,179 | 149 | 1 | 1 | 1 | 2 |

- **Director counts reproduce on `floor_area_m2` of `prepared_buildings.csv` exactly (down: ES 26, FR 6, GB 11, IT 148; up: ES 15, FR 166).** The IDF-based counts differ by 1-3 stems because the prepared figure is the sum per storey group while the IDF counts every floor surface: ES up +1 (`bb4d3f44d4fbe5b1`, 1 storey, 7 -> 1 flats, 103.6 -> 133.0 m2), FR up +3 (`3eca9e61087336f5`, `40e856fc352d5997`, `9f10cb60af487b0d`), IT down +1 (`a53d307eac631355`, 17,272 -> 15,693 m2, 16 -> 279 flats), IT up +1 (`05b52a0da00a6046`).
- **One mechanism covers both directions (down and up): the floor area of a storey is carried only if that storey holds at least one flat.** `allocate_european_dwellings` gives storey i `quotient + (1 if i < remainder)` flats (`openubem/geometry/european_residential.py:2943-2950`); when flats < storeys the top storeys get count 0 and `generate_european_building_dwelling_layout` folds each into the storey below (`storey_span + 1`, `european_residential.py:2755-2760`); `european_building_layout_to_zone_specs` then emits one zone per flat with `height_m = span x 3.0` and one polygon (`:2860-2869`), so one floor surface stands for `span` storeys. `_extrude_zone_groups_per_storey` (`scripts/run_eu_s2_campaign.py:1157-1172`) builds each run with `height = m x first["height_m"]`, `num_stories = m`, where `first` is the lowest member: the span of the last member is ignored. Result: the building keeps `min(flats, storeys)` storeys of floor. Census: of the 383 listed stems, 375 have a flat count below the storey count in the tree that lost floor (`mech` = count_below_storeys_absorbed_floors); storeys in the IDF equal the flat count in the same way for the examples below.
  - **Down (10-05 smaller; ES 26, FR 6, GB 11, IT 148; the new T06p count fell below the storey count, the old one did not):** worked example Bologna `0177dae25178d27e` (storeys 4, flats reduced to 1): 10-03 five zones `F0..F3_dwelling_0` + `F0_dwelling_1`, each 3.0 m, 111.1 m2; 10-05 one zone `F0_dwelling_0`, 12.0 m tall, one floor, 27.8 m2. Madrid `way/403855650` (stem `5bce5fa408cac4fb`): 29 -> 1 flats, 3 storeys -> 1, ratio 0.3334. GB `01b5cdbb0b015fb9`: 17 -> 2 flats, 3 storeys -> 2 (merged run, third storey's height dropped), ratio 0.6667. Two shapes: single-member run = one tall zone with one floor (tall zones in 10-05: ES 12, FR 1, GB 2, IT 50); multi-member run = lost storeys (the rest).
  - **Up (10-05 larger; ES 15, FR 169; the OLD count was below the storey count, so 10-03 and 09-08 were the short ones):** worked example Lyon `08efb1cf7304cebe` (manifest `levels` = 6): 10-03 flats 4, zones `F0..F3_dwelling_0`, 4 x 3.0 m, 449.9 m2, building 12 m tall; 10-05 flats 13, 13 zones over `F0..F5`, 18 m tall, 674.8 m2. Madrid `1e4daff30ccf7381`: 1 -> 19 flats, 1 -> 3 storeys, ratio 3.0. In all 169 Lyon up cases 10-05 storeys equal the manifest `levels`; 10-03 did not. So for these the 10-05 floor area is the faithful one and the 10-03/09-08 EUI denominators were too small.
  - **Zero-floor (IT 1: `97d478bc56840531_F0_dwelling_3`; 20 flats, 4 per storey):** not the absorbed-storey rule. Rebuilt locally (no EnergyPlus; same final IDF surfaces as the shipped one): right after `extrude_geometry` (`run_eu_s2_campaign.py:1895`) the ground floor of block `..._3` is split into `Floor 0001_1`, `_2` (about 0.001 m2 each) and `_3` (54.999 m2, a valid 9-vertex ring), but `_3` lies on the neighbouring side and shares only 2 vertices and 0.0 m2 with the flat's own triangle (55.000 m2); `_clip_zone_horizontals_to_own_polygon` (`:1057`, drop at `:1090`) removes `_3` as outside the own polygon, and `_drop_sliver_surfaces` (`:653-681`, call `:1930`) removes the two fragments. The fault is upstream, in the geomeppy intersect step inside `extrude_geometry`; the clip and sliver steps only delete what it left. It is the only zero-floor flat among 4,113 IDFs.
  - **Small remainder (8 stems, ratio 0.91-1.28, storeys unchanged):** ES `bb4d3f44d4fbe5b1`, FR `bfbc1caab1a332a4`, GB `933a2f079f1d6277`, IT `05b52a0da00a6046`, `1773a1e9f64767b1`, `97d478bc56840531` (the zero-floor one), `a53d307eac631355`, `cc55f139215783a3`: same storeys, a different flat count changes the plate partition (circulation core carved or not). Cause not traced further in part 1.
- **Deviations:** `tall_zones_05` uses a fixed 4.5 m, not 1.5 x the building's median zone height. Part 1 only; no fix proposed, part 2 not started.
- **Test status:** no code changed, no pytest run. Cross-check: census totals on `prepared_buildings.csv` reproduce the director counts; the IDF totals reproduce them within the 1-3 stems listed above.
- **Notes:** the wrong-direction check for the "up" class matters for CP-3c check 6: for those stems the 10-03 total is the wrong reference. Worked example for the up class against 09-08 not re-measured here (director measurement in FINDING 289 stands).

#### T06s part 1 — director audit (2026-10-02 13:10 EDT)

- **PASS:** census CSV has 383 rows. The per-district class counts in the progress log reproduce from the CSV. The director counts reproduce exactly on `prepared_buildings.csv`, and the IDF counts differ by 1 to 3 named stems.
- **PASS:** cited lines checked by the director: `european_residential.py:2753-2762` (a storey with 0 flats folds into the storey below as `storey_span + 1`), `:2858-2872` (one zone per flat, `height_m = span x 3.0`, one floor polygon), `:2943-2950` (`quotient + (1 if index < remainder)` gives 0 when flats < storeys), `run_eu_s2_campaign.py:1157-1172` (`height = m * first["height_m"]` ignores the last member's span).
- **PASS:** only scratch files were written and no repo code was changed.
- **Ruling (director, D-EU-143, highest accuracy; owner "continue however you recommend by considering always highest accuracy"):**
  - **Every storey keeps its own zones.** A flat that spans several storeys is modelled as one zone per storey with the same plan, the way 10-03 already built buildings where flats ≥ storeys. Each storey then has its own floor surface, its own internal floor/ceiling and its own conditioned area. One tall zone with one floor is never emitted.
  - The flat count (`dwellings_used`) does not change. It comes from `_dwellings_for` (`run_eu_s2_district_campaign.py:1185`), not from the zones.
  - Up class: 10-05 is the faithful version (storeys equal the data `levels` in all 169 Lyon cases). These stems must come out **unchanged** from the fix.
  - **Consequence recorded under FINDING 289:** the 2026-09-08 official EUIs share the up-class defect, because they were built with old counts below the storey count, which made the floor area too small and the EUI too high. This touches Lyon 169 of 522 homes and Madrid 15. The 09-08 Lyon 70.345716 *(superseded 2026-10-06 by results/EU11_results_2026-10-06, D-EU-145)* is therefore biased high. It stays the quoted number until CP-3c only because no corrected number exists yet.
  - Zero-floor flat `97d478bc56840531_F0_dwelling_3` (one flat of 20 in one IT building, about 5 % of that building's floor area): known limitation. The cause is the geomeppy intersect inside `extrude_geometry`. It is not fixed (effect on the Bologna pooled EUI is below 0.01 %). It is registered `[OPEN]` in the debug references, and the CP-3c check-6 exception lists it by name.
  - The 8 small-remainder stems (same storeys, ratio 0.91 to 1.28) come from the circulation core being carved or not when the flat count changes. That is by rule, and it is accepted as a named reason, not chased.
- **CP-3c check 6 amended (the 10-03 reference is wrong for the up class):** in every 10-06 IDF, (a) the distinct floor z levels equal the data storey count, and (b) each storey's total floor-surface area is within 1 % of the ground storey's. The check runs on all 4,113 stems, not only the rebuilt ones. Exceptions are by name only: the zero-floor stem, plus any storey whose plate is set back by rule (none known).

#### T06s part 2 — fix, rebuild, gates

**What.**
1. Fix `generate_european_building_dwelling_layout` (`openubem/geometry/european_residential.py:2753-2762`). A storey whose allocation has `dwelling_count == 0` becomes its own `EuropeanStoreyGroup(start_storey_index=index, storey_span=1, layout=<the layout of the group below>)` instead of widening the group below. Every group then has `storey_span == 1`.
2. Harden `_extrude_zone_groups_per_storey` (`scripts/run_eu_s2_campaign.py:1169-1172`): `height = sum(z["height_m"] for z in members)` and `num_stories = m`, with an `AssertionError` if any member's `storey_span != 1`.
3. Rebuild the same way T06p built 10-04 (a fresh prepare differs from the saved format in bytes, see the T06p deviation, so only the affected stems are rebuilt). The affected set per district is every building with `dwellings_used < storeys` in `<D>_win_2026-10-05/prepared_buildings.csv` (storeys from the same source the allocator uses). Every down-class stem of `t06s_floor_area_census.csv` must be in the set. Steps:
   - prepare `--only-ids <set>` into `EU-11/<D>_dw_2026-10-06/` (IT in shards as in T06p);
   - make `EU-11/<D>_win_2026-10-06a/` = a copy of `_win_2026-10-04` with those stems replaced, with `fleet.lst`, `changed_stems.txt` and `prepared_buildings.csv` updated as T06p did;
   - `python scripts/eu21/12_drop_degenerate.py --districts <D> --source-tree _win_2026-10-06a --target-tree _win_2026-10-06 --shift FR,IT --workers 8`. Allowing `10-06` as a target is the only permitted edit to that script.
   - `_win_2026-10-03/04/05` are never written.
4. Write `division_residual_2026-09-29/t06s_rebuild_md5.csv` (district, stem, md5_05, md5_06, changed).

**Why.** FINDING 289. D-EU-141 changes how a storey is cut into flats, never the building's storeys or floor area.

**How.** Same prepare entry point and arguments as T06p (`run_eu_s2_district_campaign.py`, `--only-ids`), then step 3. Local geometry only: no EnergyPlus, no ssh. Use at most 10 worker processes. Add tests to `tests/test_eu_floor_division_residual_2026_09_29.py`:
- 1 flat on 4 storeys gives 4 zones, each 3.0 m tall, each with one floor of the plate area;
- 3 flats on 4 storeys gives every storey index 0..3 present;
- the extruder assert fires on a `storey_span=2` zone.

**How to test (gates, report each pass/fail with the measured value):**
- G1. Changed stems = exactly the affected set (expected about ES 26, FR 6, GB 11, IT 149). Every other stem of `_win_2026-10-06` is byte-identical to `_win_2026-10-05`.
- G2. Amended check 6 on all 4,113 10-06 IDFs. Floor z levels equal the data storeys, and each storey's floor area is within 1 % of the ground storey's. Failures are listed by stem; the only allowed exception is `97d478bc56840531`.
- G3. Rebuilt stems: total floor area equals storeys × the ground storey's area within 1 %, and the down-class worked examples come back (Bologna `0177dae25178d27e` ≈ 111 m², Madrid `5bce5fa408cac4fb` ≈ 246 m²).
- G4. Tall zones (> 4.5 m) in 10-06 = 0.
- G5. Re-run the T06p and T06q gate scripts on the rebuilt stems. They must report the same pass state as for 10-05.
- G6. `dwellings_used` in 10-06 `prepared_buildings.csv` equals 10-05 for every stem.
- G7. The new tests pass, and the existing `tests/test_eu_floor_division_residual_2026_09_29.py` stays green.
- Then write the changed-stem list per district (`division_residual_2026-09-29/t06s_rerun_<D>.txt`). STOP and report. The director stages and submits the Speed re-runs.
- Register in `docs/docs_EXPLANATION/OpenUBEM_debug_References.md`: one fixed entry for the absorbed-storey floor loss, and one `[OPEN]` entry for the zero-floor flat.

#### T06u — `plans3D/` pages redrawn from the corrected models (owner "yes please", 2026-10-02, to the director's question "redraw the plan pages from the corrected models?")

**When.** Dispatch after the director audit of T06s part 2 passes. The pages must be ready before the T06s re-runs are submitted (memory: owner sees the plan pages before runs).
**What.** Regenerate the four `plans3D/PLANS_<D>_*.html` pages in the r2 template from `EU-11/<D>_win_2026-10-06/idfs/`, the models that are simulated. These carry walls out, windows, flat count by area, no slivers and one zone per storey.
**Why.** The current pages (`PLANS_<D>_2026-10-01.html`) are drawn from `_fix_2026-10-01` and show neither the T06p flat counts nor the T06s storeys.
**How.**
1. `scripts/eu21/09_plans3d_from_idf.py`:
   - add `--source-tag` (default the current 10-01 behaviour) and `--date` (default `2026-10-01`). With `--source-tag win_2026-10-06 --date 2026-10-06`, read only `<D>_win_2026-10-06/idfs/`, with no `_t06f` overlay. TAG reads "2026-10-06 from the simulated IDFs".
   - FR and IT IDFs are at a local origin: add `origin_offset.json` (x, y) back to every vertex before drawing, so the scene sits in map coordinates as before.
   - undivided homes: keep the T06j colour and legend from `undivided_excluded_2026-10-01.csv`.
2. Run `--all --source-tag win_2026-10-06 --date 2026-10-06` (4 local processes at most), then `--verify`.
3. Move `plans3D/PLANS_<D>_2026-10-01.html` to `plans3D/archive/PLANS_<D>_2026-10-01_fix.html`. In `plans3D/index.html`, change only the four current-page links and labels to `archive/PLANS_<D>_2026-10-06.html` "(2026-10-06)". Diff the index: only those 4 lines change.

No EnergyPlus, no Speed, no other scripts edited, no code comments.

**How to test (pass/fail per district, measured values into §8):**
- P1. Buildings on page = IDFs in the tree (ES 1,172, FR 522, GB 1,240, IT 1,179) plus the undivided count.
- P2. Every building and every storey: drawn zone area = IDF zone floor area within 0.5 %.
- P3. Embedded JSON parses; 0 keys contain `eui`; storey list length = IDF storey count; **0 storeys missing** (Bologna `0177dae25178d27e` shows 4 storeys, Madrid `5bce5fa408cac4fb` shows 3).
- P4. FR/IT position check: the centroid of 5 named buildings per district is within 1 m of the same building on the 10-01 page.
- P5. Flats drawn per building = `dwellings_used` in `<D>_win_2026-10-06/prepared_buildings.csv` for all divided homes.
- P6. Page size in MB; `index.html` diff = 4 lines.

One §8 entry `#### T06u — plan pages from 10-06 — completed YYYY-MM-DD`, then STOP.
